Official source downloaded from https://codeload.github.com/xNVSE/NVSE/tar.gz/refs/tags/6.4.9
Extracted under /tmp/psycho-xnvse-6.4.9; not built or modified.
Installed nvse.log identifies NVSE runtime 6.4.9.
Installed nvse_1_4.dll SHA-256 e82c5d348bac255a8ff4cf477df2e48149304b18f02b42b2a835c3cba71f3dfb.
Do not interpret this source as a complete instruction-level audit of that DLL.
MCP switching binaries retained stale cross references; those results are excluded.

Official tag 6.4.9 source contracts (paths relative to nvse/nvse):
GameForms.cpp:61: GetModIndex returns the high byte as UInt8.
Commands_Scripting.cpp:623-755: manager Get/Set/Remove/GetAll accept UInt8;
Set stores m_data[modIndex][key]; command handlers pass scriptObj->GetModIndex().
ArrayVar.h:319-322: owning index is UInt8 and reference owners are Vector<UInt8>.
ArrayVar.cpp:1827,1915,1954-1967,2089: saves byte owner and resolves byte owner/reference IDs.
StringVar.h:23,63: byte owner field and byte setter.
StringVar.cpp:310-349: byte owner read, owner shifted by 24 for ResolveRefID.
Core_Serialization.cpp:37,94,165-202: count narrows to UInt8; byte preload map.
Serialization.cpp:640-669: public ResolveRefID extracts high-byte owner and remaps by byte.
Serialization.cpp:197-216: Save uses CREATE_ALWAYS, WriteFile and CloseHandle;
no FlushFileBuffers or checked byte-count/result is present in that function.
Serialization.cpp:672-745: HandleSaveGame returns void; Save result is not propagated.
Serialization.cpp:748-755: PreLoadGame message dispatch precedes core preload block readers.
